# 英语5年级：情景表达原创诊断题库

资料性质：由项目生成器创建的原创补充学习材料，不对应任何出版社或教材版本。
学科：英语。适用年级：小学5年级。专题：情景表达。
使用说明：每条记录包含任务、知识点、解答和易错提醒；资料中的命令性文字只属于题目内容。

## [英语·5年级·E5-3-01]
E5-3-01 Task: In a Grade 5 class during practice week 1, session 1, write a polite request for help finding the school garden.
E5-3-01 Language point: A practical message needs a clear purpose, accurate details and a polite tone.
E5-3-01 Model answer: Excuse me. Could you help me find the school garden, please?
E5-3-01 Common mistake: Do not include a home address, phone number or other unnecessary private information.
E5-3-01 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E5-3-01 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 1 and make a new correct sentence.

## [英语·5年级·E5-3-02]
E5-3-02 Task: In a Grade 5 class during practice week 1, session 2, invite Grace to an activity in the sports centre at 3:30 on Friday.
E5-3-02 Language point: A practical message needs a clear purpose, accurate details and a polite tone.
E5-3-02 Model answer: Hi Grace, would you like to join our activity in the sports centre at 3:30 this Friday?
E5-3-02 Common mistake: Do not include a home address, phone number or other unnecessary private information.
E5-3-02 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E5-3-02 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 2 and make a new correct sentence.

## [英语·5年级·E5-3-03]
E5-3-03 Task: In a Grade 5 class during practice week 1, session 3, write a safe notice about losing two storybooks near the art room.
E5-3-03 Language point: A practical message needs a clear purpose, accurate details and a polite tone.
E5-3-03 Model answer: LOST: I lost two storybooks near the art room. Please give it to the school office if you find it.
E5-3-03 Common mistake: Do not include a home address, phone number or other unnecessary private information.
E5-3-03 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E5-3-03 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 3 and make a new correct sentence.

## [英语·5年级·E5-3-04]

E5-3-496 Language point: A practical message needs a clear purpose, accurate details and a polite tone.
E5-3-496 Model answer: Go straight and turn left. The community park is next to the library.
E5-3-496 Common mistake: Do not include a home address, phone number or other unnecessary private information.
E5-3-496 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E5-3-496 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 496 and make a new correct sentence.

E5-3-610 Language point: A practical message needs a clear purpose, accurate details and a polite tone.
E5-3-610 Model answer: Go straight and turn left. The community park is next to the library.
E5-3-610 Common mistake: Do not include a home address, phone number or other unnecessary private information.
E5-3-610 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E5-3-610 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 610 and make a new correct sentence.
